<div class="panel panel-default content-panel">
<div class="panel-heading content-heading"><div class="pull-left"><i class="fa fa-globe content-icon"></i>   </div><h2 class="content-h2">.TO Domains Worldwide</h2></div>
<div class="panel-body">
<span><b>.TO Domain Registrar - Register.TO</b> provides domain name registration services in the <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to top-level domain</a> ccTLD internationally, at the lowest prices (from <span class="emojispan">⭐ €33/yr ⭐</span> &amp; lower), in multiple currencies. The <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to TLD</a> is the country code top-level domain ( ccTLD ) to the island kingdom of Tonga. <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.TO domain names</a> may be registered by anybody; individuals &amp; businesses worldwide <a href="https://register.to/domains" title=".TO Domain Bulk Search">register .to domains</a> for various types of websites &amp; applications.  .TO domain registrants are from all countries &amp; regions of the world, including North America, South America, the UK, Europe, Africa, Asia, Australia &amp; Oceania. The <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to domain extension</a> is preferred in many cases where a short .com equivalent of a desired domain name / keyword is not available, or where 'to' has meaning in the website's language or region.  In addition to the English preposition, 'to' phonetically means 'it' in Polish, Czech &amp; Greek. Some regions may also identify with <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to</a> because it may be used to indicate a city or region such as Toronto (Canada), Torino (Italy), Tokyo (Japan), Tocantins (Brazil), or Tonga (the island kingdom of Tonga). We accept multiple currencies (US Dollar, Canadian Dollar &amp; European Euro) &amp; multiple methods of payment. <a href="/affiliate-program" title=".TO Domain Affiliate Program">.TO Domain Affiliate</a> &amp; <a href="/reseller-program" title=".TO Domain Reseller Program">.TO Domain Reseller</a> Programs are available for domain professionals, web hosts and registrars who would like to promote and/or resell <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to domain registrations</a>.</span>
</div>
</div>
</div>

<div class="panel-body">
<b>Register.TO Domain Registrar</b> <span class="emojispan c0">🚀</span> Official provider of <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">Tonga .to ccTLD</a> domain name registration services. We offer our services globally, to any interested individual, company or organization.  Our international clients include end users, web developers, domain investors, resellers &amp; other domain registrars with official domain reseller agreements with us. Our mission is to provide the world with direct, real-time <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to domain name registration</a> services &amp; specialized products related to the <a href="https://register.to/domain" title=".TO Domain Registration">.to domain name extension</a>, with customer service &amp; support at the highest priority.	</div>

jQuery.loadScript = function (url, callback) {
    jQuery.ajax({
        url: url,
        dataType: 'script',
        success: callback,
        async: true
    });
}

var scwidth = $(window).width();
$(window).on('resize', function() {
  if ($(this).width() != scwidth) {
    scwidth = $(this).width();
if(scwidth>990) {
resizeprboxes();
}
  }
});

function resizeprboxes() {
var left=$("#pricebox1a").css('height');
var right=$("#pricebox2a").css('height');
if(left>right)
{
    $("#pricebox2a").css('height',left);
    $("#pricebox2").css('height',$("#pricebox1").css('height'));

}
else
{
    $("#pricebox1a").css('height',right);
    $("#pricebox1").css('height',$("#pricebox2").css('height'));
}
}

$( document ).ready(function() {
if($(window).width()>990) {
resizeprboxes();
}
});
